Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the viruses object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 433. The moduli space has dimension at most 89 with 2 connected components.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^89(S, F) is trivial. QED.
